3.34
). We obtain

\displaystyle\rho_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}),(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})

(10.10)

\displaystyle\epsilon_{2}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}),(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})

(10.11)

\displaystyle\pi_{3}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2}),(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1}).

(10.12)

From
\rho_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})
and
\sigma_{4}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}
we deduce
(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})
. From
\epsilon_{2}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})
and
\epsilon_{2}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}
, we deduce
(\epsilon_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})
.

From
\rho_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})
and
\sigma_{4}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}
we deduce
(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})
. From
\pi_{3}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})
and
\pi_{3}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}
, we deduce
(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})
.

From
\epsilon_{2}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})
and
\sigma_{4}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{2}
we deduce
(\rho_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{2})=(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})
. From
\pi_{3}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})
and
\pi_{3}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}
, we deduce
(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})=(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})
.

Let us summarize

\displaystyle(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\epsilon_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})

(10.13)

\displaystyle(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})

(10.14)

\displaystyle(\rho_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{2})=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2}).

(10.15)

Using
(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}
inherited from (
7.3
), and
(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})
, we deduce

\displaystyle(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})

(10.16)

From
\epsilon_{1}=\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}
we know that

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\pi_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3})

(10.17)

On another part, from
\lambda=\pi_{2}\sqcup_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3}
and using (
7.3
) (if
\lambda\in({\widecheck{\mathfrak{S}}}\smallsetminus\overline{\mathfrak{S}})
) or using (
7.33
) (if
\lambda\in\overline{\mathfrak{S}}{}^{{}^{pure}}
)
, we deduce

\displaystyle(\pi_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2},\pi_{3}.

(10.18)

and then
(\epsilon_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3})=(\pi_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3})
. Let us summarize

\displaystyle(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})=(\epsilon_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3})=(\pi_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2},\pi_{3}.

(10.19)

Now, from
(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}
we have immediately

\displaystyle(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}).

(10.20)

Now, we can summarize our results. From
(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\epsilon_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})
,
(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})
and
(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})
, we obtain
(\epsilon_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})
. Hence, we have obtained

\displaystyle(\epsilon_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1}),

\displaystyle(\epsilon_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})

(10.21)

and then, in particular,

\displaystyle(\epsilon_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})\sqcup_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\epsilon_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})\sqcup_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2}).

(10.22)

On another part, we recall that, using properties (
7.52
) (
7.3
) and
\epsilon_{2}\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\pi_{2}\sqcup_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})=\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{2})
and
\pi_{3}\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\epsilon_{1}\sqcup_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})=\Theta^{{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{3})
, we have

\displaystyle\epsilon_{2}=(\epsilon_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})\sqcup_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\epsilon_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2}),

\displaystyle\pi_{3}=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})\sqcup_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})

(10.23)

We then conclude that

\displaystyle(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4})=\epsilon_{2}=\pi_{3}=(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4})

(10.24)

but this degenerate case has been excluded by assumption. As a conclusion, this configuration is excluded.

Let us now consider that
\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}\in({\check{\mathfrak{S}}}\smallsetminus\overline{\mathfrak{S}})
and
\sigma_{3},\sigma_{4}\in{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}{}^{{}^{pure}}
.
Using properties (
7.43
) and
\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{2},\sigma_{2}\asymp\sigma_{3},\sigma_{3}\asymp\sigma_{4},\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{4}
and (
7.52
)(
7.3
), we note that we can always choose
\pi_{i},\rho_{i}\in\overline{\mathfrak{S}}
for
i=1,2
such that

\displaystyle\forall i=1,2

\displaystyle\pi_{i},\rho_{i}\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{i}),\;\;\sigma_{i}=\pi_{i}\sqcup_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{i}

(10.26)

\displaystyle\pi_{1}=\pi_{2},\;\;\sigma_{3}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2},\;\;\sigma_{4}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}.

Let us now assume that there exists
\lambda\in{\mathfrak{G}}^{\check{\mathfrak{S}}}
such that
{\mathfrak{r}}^{U}(\lambda,\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2})
and
{\mathfrak{r}}^{U}(\lambda,\sigma_{3},\sigma_{4})
. We have then

\displaystyle\lambda\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2},

\displaystyle\lambda\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}).

(10.27)

The consistency relation
\sigma_{2}\asymp\sigma_{4}
means that there exists
\epsilon_{2}\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{2})
such that
\epsilon_{2}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}
.
Analogously, the consistency relation
\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{3}
means that there exists
\epsilon_{1}\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{1})
such that
\epsilon_{1}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}
.

Once again, we insist on the fact that
\epsilon_{1}\not=\pi_{1}
(resp.
\epsilon_{1}\not=\rho_{1}
) because this would mean

\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}=\epsilon_{1}=\pi_{1}=\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{2}
(resp.
\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}=\epsilon_{1}=\rho_{1}=\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}
). These degenerate cases have been excluded by assumption. We will also assume obviously
\epsilon_{2}\not=\pi_{2}
and
\epsilon_{2}\not=\rho_{2}
.

Let us now choose
\chi:=\sigma_{2}
and
\chi:=\sigma_{1}
with
\kappa:=\sigma_{3}
in (
3.34
), and simultaneously
\chi:=\sigma_{1}
and
\chi:=\sigma_{2}
with
\kappa:=\sigma_{4}
in (
3.34
). We obtain

\displaystyle\rho_{2}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}),(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{2})

(10.28)

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}),(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})

(10.29)

\displaystyle\rho_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}),(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})

(10.30)

\displaystyle\epsilon_{2}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}),(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{2})

(10.31)

From
\epsilon_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})
and
\epsilon_{1}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}
, we deduce
(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\epsilon_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})
.

From
\rho_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})
and
\sigma_{4}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}
, we deduce
(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})
.

On another part, from (
7.3
) (if
\lambda\in({\widecheck{\mathfrak{S}}}\smallsetminus\overline{\mathfrak{S}})
) or from (
7.33
) (if
\lambda\in\overline{\mathfrak{S}}{}^{{}^{pure}}
)
, we deduce

\displaystyle(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}.

(10.32)

As long as
\sigma_{2}\not=\sigma_{3}
, we have
\sigma_{3}\not\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}
. Analogously, we have
\sigma_{4}\not\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}
. Hence,

\displaystyle(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}.

(10.33)

As a result, we obtain

\displaystyle(\epsilon_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2})=(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}).

(10.34)

Hence, we have obtained a contradiction with the property (
7.4
).

As a conclusion, this configuration is excluded.

Let us now consider that
\sigma_{1},\sigma_{4}\in({\check{\mathfrak{S}}}\smallsetminus\overline{\mathfrak{S}})
and
\sigma_{2},\sigma_{3}\in{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}{}^{{}^{pure}}
.
Using properties (
7.43
) and
\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{2},\sigma_{2}\asymp\sigma_{3},\sigma_{3}\asymp\sigma_{4},\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{4}
and (
7.52
)(
7.3
), we note that we can always choose
\pi_{i},\rho_{i}\in\overline{\mathfrak{S}}
for
i=1,2
such that

\displaystyle\forall i=1,4

\displaystyle\pi_{i},\rho_{i}\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{i}),\;\;\sigma_{i}=\pi_{i}\sqcup_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{i}

(10.36)

\displaystyle\rho_{1}=\rho_{4},\;\;\sigma_{2}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1},\;\;\sigma_{3}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{4}.

Let us now assume that there exists
\lambda\in{\mathfrak{G}}^{\check{\mathfrak{S}}}
such that
{\mathfrak{r}}^{U}(\lambda,\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2})
and
{\mathfrak{r}}^{U}(\lambda,\sigma_{3},\sigma_{4})
. We have then

\displaystyle\lambda\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1}

\displaystyle\lambda\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{4}.

(10.37)

The consistency relation
\sigma_{2}\asymp\sigma_{4}
means that there exists
\epsilon_{4}\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{4})
such that
\epsilon_{4}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{2}
.
Analogously, the consistency relation
\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{3}
means that there exists
\epsilon_{1}\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{1})
such that
\epsilon_{1}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}
.

Once again, we insist on the fact that
\epsilon_{1}\not=\pi_{1}
(resp.
\epsilon_{1}\not=\rho_{1}
) because this would mean

\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}=\epsilon_{1}=\pi_{1}=\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{2}
(resp.
\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}=\epsilon_{1}=\rho_{1}=\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}
). These degenerate cases have been excluded by assumption. We will also assume obviously
\epsilon_{4}\not=\pi_{4}
and
\epsilon_{4}\not=\rho_{1}
.

Let us now choose
\chi:=\sigma_{1}
and
\chi:=\sigma_{4}
with
\kappa:=\sigma_{2}
in (
3.34
), and simultaneously
\chi:=\sigma_{1}
and
\chi:=\sigma_{4}
with
\kappa:=\sigma_{3}
in (
3.34
). We obtain

\displaystyle\pi_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}),(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})

(10.38)

\displaystyle\epsilon_{4}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{4}),(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})

(10.39)

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1}),(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})

(10.40)

\displaystyle\pi_{4}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}),(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{4}).

(10.41)

From
\pi_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})
and
\pi_{1}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{2}
, we deduce
(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})=(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})
.

From
\pi_{4}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})
and
\pi_{4}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}
, we deduce
(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})=(\pi_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})
.

We have then obtained

\displaystyle\epsilon_{4}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}),

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\pi_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}).

(10.42)

From

\displaystyle(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1},\rho_{1},

\displaystyle(\pi_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{4},\rho_{1}

(10.43)

we deduce

\displaystyle(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{4})=(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1},

\displaystyle(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\pi_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}.

(10.44)

On another part, from
\lambda\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1},\pi_{4}
, we know that
\lambda\not\sqsupseteq_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}
because, using (
7.52
), we would deduce that
\lambda=(\rho_{1}\sqcup_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1})=\sigma_{1}
and
\lambda=(\rho_{1}\sqcup_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{4})=\sigma_{4}
which has been excluded. Then, we deduce, using
(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}
and
(\pi_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}

\displaystyle(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})=(\pi_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})=(\lambda\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1})

(10.45)

As a result, we obtain

\displaystyle(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{4})=(\pi_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}),

\displaystyle(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon_{1})=(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}).

(10.46)

Hence, we have obtained a contradiction with the property (
7.4
).

As a conclusion, this configuration is excluded.

Let us now consider that
\sigma_{1},\sigma_{3}\in({\check{\mathfrak{S}}}\smallsetminus\overline{\mathfrak{S}})
and
\sigma_{2},\sigma_{4}\in{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}{}^{{}^{pure}}
.
Using properties (
7.43
) and
\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{2},\sigma_{2}\asymp\sigma_{3},\sigma_{3}\asymp\sigma_{4},\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{4}
and (
7.52
), we note that we can always choose
\pi_{i},\rho_{i}\in\overline{\mathfrak{S}}
for
i=1,2
such that

\displaystyle\forall i=1,3

\displaystyle\pi_{i},\rho_{i}\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{i}),\;\;\sigma_{i}=\pi_{i}\sqcup_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{i}

(10.49)

\displaystyle\sigma_{4}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1},\;\;\sigma_{4}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3}

\displaystyle\sigma_{2}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{3},\;\;\sigma_{2}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1}.

Let us now assume that there exists
\lambda\in{\mathfrak{G}}^{\check{\mathfrak{S}}}
such that
{\mathfrak{r}}^{U}(\lambda,\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2})
and
{\mathfrak{r}}^{U}(\lambda,\sigma_{3},\sigma_{4})
.
We will exclude the trivial case
\pi_{1}=\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{2}=\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}=\pi_{3}
, i.e. we will assume
\pi_{1}\not=\pi_{3}
. We will also exclude the trivial cases
\pi_{1}=\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{2}=\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}=\rho_{3}
or
\pi_{3}=\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}=\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}=\rho_{1}
, i.e. we will then assume
\pi_{1}\not=\rho_{3}
and
\pi_{3}\not=\rho_{1}
.

We have then

\displaystyle\lambda\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1},\pi_{3}.

(10.50)

The consistency relation
\sigma_{2}\asymp\sigma_{4}
contains nothing.
On another part, the consistency relation
\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{3}
means that there exists
\epsilon\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{1})\cap\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{3})
.

Let us now choose
\chi:=\sigma_{1}
and
\chi:=\sigma_{3}
with
\kappa:=\sigma_{2}
in (
3.34
), and simultaneously
\chi:=\sigma_{3}
and
\chi:=\sigma_{1}
with
\kappa:=\sigma_{4}
in (
3.34
).

\displaystyle(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}),(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)\sqsubseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1}

(10.51)

\displaystyle(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3}),(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)\sqsubseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{3}

(10.52)

\displaystyle(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{3}),(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)\sqsubseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3}

(10.53)

\displaystyle(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1}),(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)\sqsubseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}

(10.54)

From
(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)\sqsubseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1}
,
(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)\sqsubseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{3}
and
\sigma_{2}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{3}
and
\sigma_{2}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1}
(and also
(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)\sqsubseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3}
,
(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)\sqsubseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}
and
\sigma_{4}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho_{1}
and
\sigma_{4}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3}
) we deduce

\displaystyle(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)=(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)=(\rho_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)

(10.55)

\displaystyle(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)=(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon).

(10.56)

On another part, from
\lambda\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1},\pi_{3}
, we know that
\lambda\not\sqsupseteq_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon
because, using (
7.52
), we would deduce that
\sigma_{1}=(\epsilon\sqcup_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{1})=(\epsilon\sqcup_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{3})=\sigma_{3}
which has been excluded. Then, we deduce, using
(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon
and
(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon

\displaystyle(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)=(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)=(\lambda\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)

(10.57)

We obtain finally

\displaystyle(\pi_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)=(\rho_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon),

\displaystyle(\pi_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)=(\rho_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)

(10.58)

which contradicts (
7.4
).

As a conclusion, this configuration is excluded.

Let us now consider that
\sigma_{1}\in({\check{\mathfrak{S}}}\smallsetminus\overline{\mathfrak{S}})
and
\sigma_{2},\sigma_{3},\sigma_{4}\in{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}{}^{{}^{pure}}
.
Using properties (
7.43
) and
\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{2},\sigma_{2}\asymp\sigma_{3},\sigma_{3}\asymp\sigma_{4},\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{4}
and (
7.52
)(
7.3
), we note that we can always choose
\pi,\rho\in\overline{\mathfrak{S}}
such that

\displaystyle\pi,\rho\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{1}),\;\;\sigma_{1}=\pi\sqcup_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho

(10.60)

\displaystyle\sigma_{4}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho,\;\;\sigma_{2}\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi

.

(10.61)

Let us now assume that there exists
\lambda\in{\mathfrak{G}}^{\check{\mathfrak{S}}}
such that
{\mathfrak{r}}^{U}(\lambda,\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2})
and
{\mathfrak{r}}^{U}(\lambda,\sigma_{3},\sigma_{4})
. We have then

\displaystyle\lambda\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi,

\displaystyle\lambda\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsubseteq$}}\,_{{}_{{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}).

(10.62)

The consistency relation
\sigma_{1}\asymp\sigma_{3}
means that there exists
\epsilon\in\Theta^{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}(\sigma_{1})
such that
\epsilon\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}
.
The consistency relation
\sigma_{2}\asymp\sigma_{4}
contains nothing.

Once again, we insist on the fact that
\epsilon\not=\pi
(resp.
\epsilon\not=\rho
) because this would mean

\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}=\epsilon=\pi=\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{2}
(resp.
\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{3}=\epsilon=\rho=\sigma_{1}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{4}
). These degenerate cases have been excluded by assumption.

Let us now choose
\chi:=\sigma_{1}
with
\kappa:=\sigma_{2}
in (
3.34
), and simultaneously
\chi:=\sigma_{1}
with
\kappa:=\sigma_{3}
in (
3.34
), and simultaneously
\chi:=\sigma_{1}
with
\kappa:=\sigma_{4}
in (
3.34
). We obtain

\displaystyle\pi\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho),(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon)

(10.63)

\displaystyle\epsilon\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi),(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho)

(10.64)

\displaystyle\rho\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi),(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon).

(10.65)

From
\pi\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho)
and
\pi\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\sigma_{2}
, we deduce
(\sigma_{2}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho)=(\rho\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)
.

From
\rho\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)
and
\sigma_{4}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\rho
, we deduce
(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)=(\rho\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)
.

From
\epsilon\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)
and
\sigma_{3}\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\epsilon
, we deduce
(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)=(\epsilon\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)
.

On another part, from (
7.3
) (if
\lambda\in({\widecheck{\mathfrak{S}}}\smallsetminus\overline{\mathfrak{S}})
) or from (
7.33
) (if
\lambda\in\overline{\mathfrak{S}}{}^{{}^{pure}}
)
, we deduce

\displaystyle(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi.

(10.66)

As long as
\sigma_{2}\not=\sigma_{3}
, we have
\sigma_{3}\not\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}
. Analogously, we have
\sigma_{4}\not\sqsupseteq_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi_{2}
. Hence,

\displaystyle(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)=(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)=(\sigma_{3}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}(\sigma_{4}\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)\raisebox{5.59721pt}{\,\rotatebox{180.0}{$\sqsupseteq$}}\,_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi.

(10.67)

As a result, we obtain

\displaystyle(\epsilon\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi)=(\rho\sqcap_{{}_{\overline{\mathfrak{S}}}}\pi).

(10.68)

Hence, we have obtained a contradiction with the property (
7.4
).

As a conclusion, this configuration is excluded.

As a conclusion of the previous analysis we obtain that
\forall i\in\{1,2,3,4\},\sigma_{i}\in\overline{\mathfrak{S}}{}^{{}^{pure}}
.
